Use whenever an agent runs unattended or with --dangerously-skip-permissions, before letting an agent near a repo you care about, after an agent has proposed or run a destructive git command, or when someone asks how to stop an AI from deleting their work. - **destructive-op-preflight**: Use when the next step could destroy work nothing can bring back: wiping the tree for a clean slate, discarding uncommitted or untracked changes, deleting generated or scaffolding files, dropping local commits never pushed to sync a branch with its remote, or overwriting what the remote already has. Also when a safety flag just refused. Do NOT use for reversible work: ordinary edits, commits, non-force pushes, dry runs that print what would happen, or history edits the reflog can undo. ([ContextLab/claude-skill-compounder](https://github.com/ContextLab/claude-skill-compounder/tree/HEAD/skills/destructive-op-preflight) · ★1)
- **destructive-operation-guard**: CRITICAL SAFETY SKILL - Always active. Use this skill to prevent destructive operations without explicit user confirmation. This skill MUST be consulted before executing potentially harmful commands. ([latestaiagents/agent-skills](https://github.com/latestaiagents/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/safety/ai-safety-guardrails/destructive-operation-guard) · ★5)
- **destructive-ops**: Run before deleting any file, dropping or truncating anything in a database, overwriting files you did not generate, force-pushing, or staging binary or large (&gt;1MB) files.
